placebo	5176	4276	2953	1677	480
rivaroxaban 2.5	5174	4264	2948	1633	475
rivaroxaban 5	5176	4195	2840	1599	465
Legend: Solid line = dose = 0; Dashed line = dose = 2.5; Dotted line = dose = 5.

L2577: Figure 6 illustrates the metabolic pathways of Ivabradine (S 18982), showing various metabolites (M1 through M32) and the enzymes involved in their formation.
The central metabolite, S 18982 (M29), is formed via N-desmethylation of the parent drug (M28) by S16257. S 18982 undergoes further metabolism:
N-desmethylation: Leads to M28 (M29 \xrightarrow{\text{S16257}} M28).
O-desmethylation: Leads to M22.
Hydroxylation: Leads to M25.
Other major pathways include:
M28 Metabolism:O-desmethylation: Leads to M22.

L2579: Diagram illustrating the SHIFT Study Plan timeline. The study duration is 52 weeks, divided into a Run-in period and a Follow-up period.
The timeline shows visits occurring every two weeks (Run-in) and every four months (Follow-up period).
Visits include: ASSE V visit, D000 V visit, D014 V visit, D028 V visit, M004 V visit, M008 V visit, M012 V visit, M016 V visit, M020 V visit, M024 V visit, M028 V visit, M032 V visit, M036 V visit, M040 V visit, M044 V visit, M048 V visit, M052 V visit, and a Termination visit.
The study compares Ivabradine (Starting dose 5 mg twice daily, then 7.5 mg, 5 mg or 2.5 mg daily, N = 3250) against Matching placebo twice daily (N = 3250).
L2580: Flowchart illustrating the SHIFT Adjudication Process:
PSE leads to EVC Member 1 Electronic adjudication and EVC Member 2 Electronic adjudication.
EVC Member 1 and EVC Member 2 perform a Comparison.
The comparison results in Agreement or Disagreement.
If Agreement, the process proceeds to Adjudicated PSE.
If Disagreement, the process proceeds to EVC member 3 Electronic adjudication.
EVC member 3 Electronic adjudication leads to two outcomes:EVC member 3 agrees with EVC member 1 or 2, which leads to Adjudicated PSE.
EVC member 3 disagrees with both EVC members 1 & 2, which leads to EVC Consensus Meeting.

EVC Consensus Meeting leads to Adjudicated PSE.
Additionally, EVC member creates new PSE, requests discussion, which leads to EVC Consensus Meeting.
L2581: Flowchart illustrating Information Communication Within SHIFT.
INVESTIGATOR (Fills in e-CRF AE/PSE page) triggers an Automatic e-mail to the MONITORING STRUCTURE.
MONITORING STRUCTURE (Monitor and Medical Reviewer) sends the PSE file to the PSE CRO.
PSE CRO activities include:
Design of a specific PSE database
Design of the user manual; organisation of the hotline
Tracking of PSE documents
Scan copies of source documents
Upload scanned documents in the PSE database
Track and review the PSE files.
If necessary, queries to monitor (delayed file, missing documents, additional information requested by EVC member...)
Selection of 2 EVC members
Accessibility of completed PSE files to EVC members via a website
Organisation and attendance to face-to-face meetings (In case of mismatching at the first level of adjudication: consensus adjudication)
The PSE CRO sends Queries to the Monitoring Structure, which answers them. The Monitoring Structure requests English translation if not done.
The PSE CRO sends the PSE file to the EVC MEMBERS (e-mail alert to 2 EVC members: PSE File to be adjudicated within 10 working days).
EVC MEMBERS activities include:
Review the PSE file
Adjudicate PSE (electronic adjudication)
Request for additional information or narrative*
Attend face-to-face meetings (consensus electronic adjudication)
EVC MEMBERS send Electronic adjudication and Additional Information requested (if any) back to the PSE CRO.
The PSE CRO sends e-mail alert in case of delayed adjudication to the EVC MEMBERS.
Data transfer of adjudication results and Edition of Data Clarification Form (if needed) occur between the PSE CRO and the I.R.I.S. DATA MANAGEMENT DEPARTMENT (Edition of Data Clarification Form (if any), Validation of data for final analysis).
I.R.I.S. DT: PSE and I.R.I.S PV: PSE/SAE are notified in ARGUS.
Narratives*: forwarded to the EVC members upon request only; in this case, the baseline HR values will not be disclosed.

L2583: Diagram illustrating the Communications Flows within BEAUTIFUL.
Legend:
① IMMEDIATE NOTIFICATION at awareness
② DOCUMENTATION PROCESS: Initial PSE File within 30 days of PSE awareness/ Complementary documentation within 10 working days following (b) (4)
Flowchart Description:
INVESTIGATOR Notifies the occurrence of a PSE and collects the Source Documents required for the PSE file.
① Fax PSE notification leads to MONITORING STRUCTURE.
MONITORING STRUCTURE performs a monitoring visit, prepares the PSE file including anonymous photocopies of source documents and their English translations, copies of the AE and Concomitant Treatment CRF pages, and creates a narrative.
② Collect PSE documentation leads to TRACKING OF PSE DOCUMENTS.
TRACKING OF PSE DOCUMENTS (b) (4) Collects and reviews the PSE files. If no PSE file received within 30 days, or if documents are missing or outstanding, (b) (4) will send queries to the site via the monitoring structure within 1 week.
TRACKING OF PSE DOCUMENTS (b) (4) prepares one or more monthly binders including complete PSE files with 1 Submission list + 1 Adjudication Form A per PSE to be adjudicated + Patient Case Summaries (PCS*) and results of previous adjudications (if any). Send alerts and/or Data Clarification Forms (DCF) in the case of results not received in time or inconsistent data on results.
TRACKING OF PSE DOCUMENTS (b) (4) prepares re-adjudication binders in view of face-to-face meetings.
TRACKING OF PSE DOCUMENTS (b) (4) attends face-to-face meetings.
TRACKING OF PSE DOCUMENTS (b) (4) ships adjudication results to DATA MANAGEMENT DEPARTMENT.
DATA MANAGEMENT DEPARTMENT (b) (4) performs data entry of adjudication results, validation of data, edition of DCF.
DATA MANAGEMENT DEPARTMENT (b) (4) sends completed Adjudication Forms A/B to TRACKING OF PSE DOCUMENTS.
TRACKING OF PSE DOCUMENTS (b) (4) sends Edition of DCF if needed to DATA MANAGEMENT DEPARTMENT.
Monthly adjudication binders sent to the 3 EVC members.
EVC MEMBERS Review the PSE files, adjudicate PSE and complete Adjudication Forms A. Request for additional information or narrative** if needed. Attend face-to-face meetings. Participate in re-adjudication process.
Completed Adjudication Forms A returned within 2 weeks leads to EVC MEMBERS.
DCF sent if needed leads to EVC MEMBERS.
Footnotes:
PCS*: PCS forwarded to the adjudicators will not display the HR values obtained from the resting ECG recordings performed during the visits.
Narratives**: Upon request, a patient narrative will be forwarded to the adjudicators. In this case, the HR values obtained on resting ECG during the study visits will not be disclosed.

Forest plot showing Hazard Ratios (HR) and 95% Confidence Intervals (CI) for CV Deaths by Center Enrollments. The plot compares sites enrolling 1 subject versus sites enrolling more than 1 subject. The HR for 1 subject is 0.11 (CI: 0.02, 0.64, P=0.014), indicating a statistically significant reduction in CV deaths. The HR for sites enrolling > 1 subject is 0.92 (CI: 0.81, 1.05, P=0.196), indicating no significant difference.
L2587: Flowchart illustrating the disposition of subjects in the SHIFT trial:
Screened (N = 7411) leads to Selected (N = 7106) and Not Selected (n = 305).
Selected (N = 7106) leads to Randomised (N = 6558) and Not included/non randomised (n = 547)a.
Randomised (N = 6558) leads to Randomised Set (N = 6505) and Included/not randomised (n = 1) (063 643 1441 00278).
Randomised Set (N = 6505) leads to Ivabradine (N = 3241) and Placebo (N = 3264).
Ivabradine (N = 3241) leads to Safety set = 3232 (9 patients took no medication and were excluded from safety analysisc), Consent withdrawal (n = 73), and Lost to follow up (n = 2).
Placebo (N = 3264) leads to Safety set = 3260 (5 patients took no medication and were excluded from safety analysisc; 1 patient who was not randomised, but dispensed placebo was included in the safety analysis), Consent withdrawal (n = 58), and Lost to follow up (n = 1).
Both Ivabradine and Placebo groups lead to Analysed for efficacy criteria: (N = 3241) and (N = 3264).

L2589: Figure 22 is a Kaplan-Meier curve illustrating the cumulative frequency of permanent study drug withdrawal over time (months) for two treatment groups: Ivabradine (solid line) and Placebo (dashed line).

The Ivabradine group shows a cumulative withdrawal frequency of approximately 30% at 42 months, while the Placebo group shows a cumulative withdrawal frequency of approximately 32% at 42 months. The curves are very close, suggesting similar rates of withdrawal between the two groups.

L2608: Figure 43 is a line graph illustrating the Hazard Ratio (HR) and 95% Confidence Interval (CI) for hospitalization due to Worsening Heart Failure (WHF) in the SHIFT trial, stratified by Baseline Heart Rate (HR) cutoffs. The X-axis represents Baseline Heart Rate Cutoffs (71, 75, 77, 80, 85, 90, 95, 100 bpm). The Y-axis represents HR and 95% CI, ranging from 0.4 to 2.8.
The graph shows two lines: one for patients with Baseline HR Greater than the Cutoff (blue dashed line) and one for patients with Baseline HR Less than the Cutoff (red solid line). The legend indicates: Abovec (blue dashed line), Greater than Cutoff (blue dashed line), and Less than Cutoff HR (red solid line).
For the "Less than Cutoff HR" group (red solid line), the HR decreases as the cutoff increases. At a cutoff of 71 bpm, the HR is approximately 2.7. At a cutoff of 75 bpm, the HR is approximately 1.0. For cutoffs of 77 bpm and higher, the HR is slightly below 1.0, ranging from approximately 0.8 to 0.9.
For the "Greater than Cutoff" group (blue dashed line), the HR is consistently below 1.0 across all cutoffs, ranging from approximately 0.7 to 0.8.
The graph demonstrates that the hazard ratio for WHF hospitalization is significantly reduced (HR < 1.0) when the baseline heart rate is less than the cutoff, particularly for cutoffs of 75 bpm or higher.

L2620: Figure 55 is a dual-axis plot illustrating the FDA SHIFT Analysis. The X-axis represents Events' dates, spanning from 05/07/07 to 06/17/08. The left Y-axis represents the p-value (ranging from 0.0 to 0.8), and the right Y-axis represents the # of events (ranging from 0 to 1500).
The plot shows three main components:
A jagged line representing the distribution of Cox PH model p-values, which fluctuates significantly, peaking around 0.8 near 09/09/07.

The total number of events increases steadily, reaching approximately 1500 by 06/17/08. The Ivabradine Events are consistently lower, reaching approximately 1000 by 06/17/08.
A horizontal dashed line near the bottom of the p-value axis, likely indicating a significance threshold (e.g., p=0.05).
Annotations include "Placebo Events" pointing to the p-value line, and "Total # of Events" and "Ivabradine Events" pointing to the cumulative event areas.
